```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

```java
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;

            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();

        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;

            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
```

```java
public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```java
public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;

        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

```

```java
    //@ requires \typeof(adjacencyMat) == \type(int[][]);
    //@ requires \forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat.length; adjacencyMat[i] != null && adjacencyMat[i].length == adjacencyMat.length;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat.length; i != j ==> adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= nVert;
        //@ maintaining \forall int x, y; 0 <= x && x < i && x < nVert && 0 <= y && y < nVert && y < i; adjacencyMat[x][y] == adjacencyMat[y][x];
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases nVert - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= nVert;
```
            //@ maintaining \forall int x, y; 0 - 1 <= x && x < i && x <= nVert && 0 <= y && y < j && y < nVert; adjacencyMat[x][y] == adjacencyMat[y][x];  
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;
            //@ decreases nVert - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    //@ behaviors complete;

    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
